- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Elizabeth, leader of England (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: England
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Ship of the Line, Replacing Frigate (Requires technology: Navigation)
        - Unique Unit: Redcoat, Replacing Fusilier (Requires technology: Rifling)
        - Unique Building: White Tower, Replacing Ironworks (Requires technology: Machinery)
        - Unique Building: Steam Mill, Replacing Factory (Requires technology: Steam Power)
        - Civilization Ability: -25% Naval Unit Gold maintenance. Military Naval Units start with the Sun Never Sets Promotion. Diplomatic Units and Archaeologists may enter rival territory without Open Borders.
      - Leader: Elizabeth
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Exploitation (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Food and +1 Production from Farms, Camps, and Plantations.
        - * +1 Production and Science from Coasts, Lakes, and Oceans.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Imperium (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * Cities gain 20 Production and Gold when their borders expand, scaling with Era (40 if all Authority policies are adopted).
        - * Receive 40 Science and Culture when you found or conquer Cities, scaling with Era. Conquest bonus also scales based on City population.
      - Dominance (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * All Melee Units gain the Endurance Promotion (Heal 15 HP after defeating an Enemy Unit).
        - * +1 Production in every City for every 10 Military Units in Empire.
        - * +5 Military Supply.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Rationalism (New Branch):
        - Rationalism focuses on maximizing Growth and Science output.
        - Adopting Rationalism grants:
        - * +2 Production and +3 Science from all Strategic Resources.
        - * +5% Science in all Cities.
        - Each Rationalism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +2% Science.
        - Adopting all Policies in Rationalism grants:
        - * Unlocks building Bletchley Park.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +12 City Security in every City.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Scientists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 10
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 2
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 3
        - Boldness: 10
        - WarBias: 10
        - HostileBias: 10
        - WarmongerHate: 10
        - NeutralBias: 2
        - FriendlyBias: 5
        - GuardedBias: 3
        - AfraidBias: 1
        - DiplomaticBalance: 8
        - Friendliness: 3
        - WorkWithWillingness: 6
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 10
        - Loyalty: 1
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 7
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 4
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 5
        - MinorCivWarBias: 8
        - DenounceWillingness: 8
        - Forgiveness: 3
        - Meanness: 9
        - Neediness: 4
        - Chattiness: 1
        - DeceptiveBias: 3
        - Rationale: TOTAL WAR FOR VICTORY PROTECTION: Maximum aggression maintained against Netherlands to secure capitulation before Cultural Victory. Chattiness 1 prevents diplomatic distractions during critical 10-turn Citizen Earth Protocol construction. Meanness 9 maintains brutal pressure on Dutch. Minor civ friendly bias 7 preserves critical alliances (Kyiv, Almaty). No compromises while victory is within reach.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: None
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Industry (New Branch)
        - Rationale: INDUSTRY BRANCH FOR ECONOMIC STABILIZATION: Happiness at 44% requires immediate attention. Industry opener provides +2 Trade Routes (critical for GPT and happiness generation). Industry finisher grants +3 Happiness per unique luxury and +2 Production/Gold from specialists - essential for maintaining empire stability during final Cultural Victory push. Better immediate economic choice than Rationalism given happiness crisis.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Prague
        - Public: 80
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: Prague remains critical after flipping from Iroquois control. Currently Friends with Iroquois at 53 influence - maintaining high priority ensures we consolidate this gain and prevent Iroquois from regaining foothold in northern region. Protestant majority helps secure position.
        - UpdatedTurn: 194
        
        ### Djenne
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Djenne is effectively lost. Both Morocco and Iroquois used Great Diplomats to contest this city-state, and the Iroquois now lead at 183 influence while I'm at -25. With my influence negative and two Great Diplomats spent against me, this is not recoverable without massive resources better spent on Yerevan. Clearing priority to accept this loss and focus on winnable contests.
        - UpdatedTurn: 287
        
        ### Ban Chiang
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: Ban Chiang is the site of Indonesia's Open Door proposal. Critical to maintain high priority here to block Indonesia's Open Door proposal which would reset influence and prevent alliances. Must secure position before World Congress vote to deny Indonesia control of this city-state.
        - UpdatedTurn: 336
        
        ### Yerevan
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Yerevan is effectively lost - Indonesia dominates at 624 influence versus my -55. Their quest for Protestantism conversion offers only 168 influence, insufficient to overcome Indonesia's lead. Diplomatic resources better spent on military operations against Indonesian cities. Accepting defeat here to concentrate on capturing Jakarta/Surabaya before Indonesia achieves cultural victory in 5 turns.
        - UpdatedTurn: 345
        
        ### Bucharest
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 40
        - Rationale: Bucharest reduced priority - Netherlands established strong lead at 424 vs my 310. Gap too large to overcome without massive gold investment better spent securing Helsinki. Maintaining minimal presence preserves option if Netherlands neglects alliance.
        - UpdatedTurn: 369
        
        ### England
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Self-reference: England. Relationship maintained at maximum commitment to total war strategy.
        - UpdatedTurn: 385
        
        ### America
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: America relationship maintained at neutral 0/0. They are my vassal through capitulation - cannot threaten me for remaining turns of peace treaty. Vassal status provides 12 votes in World Congress. Neutral stance reflects vassal relationship and allows diplomatic flexibility.
        - UpdatedTurn: 385
        
        ### Hanoi
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: Hanoi maintained at priority 95. Ally status secured with France at 192 influence and protected status. Protestantism majority strengthens position. Eastern approach secure against Iroquois, serving as military/diplomatic support base with active trade routes.
        - UpdatedTurn: 385
        
        ### Perge
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 40
        - Rationale: Perge reduced to priority 40. Netherlands Sphere of Influence proposal enacted - permanent ally status for Netherlands. My influence only 5 vs their 273. Reduced priority acknowledges permanent Dutch control while maintaining minimal presence.
        - UpdatedTurn: 385
        
        ### Shanghai
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 40
        - Rationale: Shanghai maintained at reduced priority 40. Netherlands Sphere of Influence enacted - permanent ally status established. My influence 45 vs their 329. Maintaining minimal presence acknowledges Dutch permanent control while preserving option if situation changes.
        - UpdatedTurn: 385
        
        ### France
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: France relationship maintained at 100/100. Napoleon remains strongest World Congress ally with 5 votes. Defensive Pact renewed this turn (turn 395) for 50 turns with 7 GPT. France's war with Iroquois prevents Hiawatha from interfering with Indonesian theater. Open borders maintained for operational flexibility. Critical ally for post-Indonesia diplomatic positioning.
        - UpdatedTurn: 396
        
        ### Morocco
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 90
        - Rationale: Morocco alliance maintained at 90/90. Defensive Pact secures southern flank against Iroquois. Their 3 votes support World Congress position. Morocco provides stable diplomatic anchor, allowing concentration on Indonesian theater. Religious differences (-30) manageable given strong diplomatic ties and shared enemy in Indonesia.
        - UpdatedTurn: 396
        
        ### Indonesia
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Indonesia down to Medan only. Maintain absolute hostility until Medan captured and Indonesia eliminated or capitulated. Cannot allow recovery - they remain culturally influential over 5 civs.
        - UpdatedTurn: 407
        
        ### Utica
        - Public: 20
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: UTICA LOST TO NETHERLANDS GREAT DIPLOMAT: Netherlands expended Great Diplomat to flip Utica from me (was ally at 228 influence, now Netherlands leads 514 to -60). Gap insurmountable without massive diplomatic capital better spent on Helsinki World Congress delegate. Reducing from 95 to 20 acknowledges strategic loss while preserving minimal monitoring. Culture quest expires in 8 turns offering 389 influence insufficient to overcome Dutch lead.
        - UpdatedTurn: 413
        
        ### The Huns
        - Public: 70
        - Private: 70
        - Rationale: HUNNIC COOPERATION POST-INDONESIA: Indonesia eliminated, reducing immediate need for Hunnic military support, but Attila remains useful ally for regional security against potential Dutch-Iroquois alignment. 70/70 maintains cooperative stance without overcommitting diplomatic capital needed for nuclear program.
        - UpdatedTurn: 415
        
        ### Kyiv
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: KYIV CEMENT ALLIANCE: Just flipped to England via Agent Scarlett election rigging (368 influence, Ally). Critical northern anchor against Iroquois and Netherlands expansion. Must maintain maximum priority to secure this gain and complete road quest for additional influence bonus.
        - UpdatedTurn: 415
        
        ### Almaty
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: HELSINKI MAXIMUM PRIORITY - WORLD CONGRESS VOTING IN 6 TURNS: Agent Gwendolyn election rigging critical. Netherlands leads influence 185 to my 35. This delegate essential to close gap with Dutch 20 delegates versus my 15. Must secure through espionage - no cost spared for strategic city-state that provides edge in United Nations vote. Victory in World Congress prevents Dutch diplomatic victory.
        - UpdatedTurn: 417
        
        ### The Netherlands
        - Public: -80
        - Private: -90
        - Rationale: MAXIMUM HOSTILITY AGAINST NETHERLANDS: War continues with -76% warscore for Netherlands. They recaptured Nijmegen but I immediately recaptured it back. York's Citizen Earth Protocol (8 turns) is vulnerable to Dutch atomic attack from Groningen. Must maintain extreme pressure to prevent them from striking York or The Hague. Need capitulation or elimination.
        - UpdatedTurn: 422
        
        ### The Iroquois
        - Public: 30
        - Private: 25
        - Rationale: IROQUOIS PEACE MANAGEMENT: Peace treaty with Iroquois expires imminently. Increasing from 30/20 to 30/25 - still neutral-guarded but slightly less hostile. Do not want two-front war while protecting Citizen Earth Protocol construction. Hiawatha occupied with Morocco/France war provides buffer. Monitor for peace treaty expiration.
        - UpdatedTurn: 422
        
        ### Helsinki
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: HELSINKI MAXIMUM PRIORITY - WORLD CONGRESS VOTING IN 1 TURN: Agent Gwendolyn election rigging critical. Netherlands leads influence 266 to my 123. This delegate could determine United Nations vote outcome - Netherlands has 19 delegates vs my 15. Must secure through espionage to block Dutch diplomatic victory attempt.
        - UpdatedTurn: 422
        
        ### Edinburgh
        - Public: 75
        - Private: 75
        - Rationale: EDINBURGH RECOVERY PRIORITY: Iroquois leads at 237 vs my 210, but gap closing. Culture quest expires in 7 turns offering 188 influence and 562 culture - valuable for Citizen Earth Protocol construction. Priority 75 maintains pressure to reclaim this city-state.
        - UpdatedTurn: 422
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: CULTURAL VICTORY PROTECTION - Citizen Earth Protocol 8 turns from completion. Maximum Culture/Wonder priority ensures completion. Nuke/UseNuke at 100/95 to deter Dutch atomic attack on York. Science 95 for Advanced Ballistics (nuclear missiles in 8 turns as backup deterrent). CityDefense 90 protects York. Happiness 80 addresses 45% crisis. Reduced Offense to 70 as consolidation sufficient after Nijmegen recapture. Diplomacy 40 maintains World Congress relevance with voting in 1 turn.
        - GrandStrategy: Culture

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 43
        - Status: World Congress - Voting Now
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Ban Luxury: Whales:
            - Description: A Luxury resource is designated as immoral, endangered, or otherwise inappropriate for use by the general population. As long as the ban is in place, Civilizations receive no Happiness or Monopoly benefits from this Luxury resource.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Sanction: The Huns:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Endowment for the Arts:
            - Description: Increases up to 33% generation of Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians. Decreases up to 33% generation of Great Scientists, Engineers, and Merchants.
              Civilizations with Scores from Policies and Great Works below the global median will receive a boost to Production and Culture up to 33%.
              Details: The boosts are proportional to the difference between the score of the Civilization and the leader.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: 412
          - Open Door: Edinburgh:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Dutch Sphere of Influence: Shanghai:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Dutch Sphere of Influence: Perge:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Dutch Sphere of Influence: Bucharest:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: 412
          - World Religion: Confucianism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Historical Landmarks:
            - Description: Great Person tile improvements provide +1 to Food, Production, and Gold when worked, and Landmarks built by Archaeologists provide +1 Science, Culture, and Faith when worked.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Archaeology.
            - Proposer: England
          - Enact: Decolonization:
            - Description: Enforce global decolonization. If successful, the Influence of all Players with all City-States is lowered to 50, unless they are permanent allies.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Radio.
            - Proposer: America
          - Enact: United Nations:
            - Description: Begins the United Nations project. Once underway, Civilizations can contribute Production towards its completion by selecting it in the city production list. When complete, Civilizations receive bonuses based on how much they contributed.
              : United Nations wonder appears in Capital. (Receive 1 Additional Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World. Also, when you expend a Great Person, earn 25 Influence with all City-States you have met. Triggers a Golden Age, as well as the United Nations Special Session of the World Congress if the world has reached the Atomic Era.)
              : Receive an immediate boost of 25 Influence with all City-States. The Consulate National Wonder appears in Capital. (Receive 1 Additional Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World.)
              : Receive a free Great Diplomat.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - Proposer: The Netherlands

        ### 430
        - Type: PlayerDoneTurn
        - NextPlayer: 3: France
        - Player: 2: England
        
        You, Elizabeth (leader of England, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 423.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Elizabeth, leader of England (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: England
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Ship of the Line, Replacing Frigate (Requires technology: Navigation)
        - Unique Unit: Redcoat, Replacing Fusilier (Requires technology: Rifling)
        - Unique Building: White Tower, Replacing Ironworks (Requires technology: Machinery)
        - Unique Building: Steam Mill, Replacing Factory (Requires technology: Steam Power)
        - Civilization Ability: -25% Naval Unit Gold maintenance. Military Naval Units start with the Sun Never Sets Promotion. Diplomatic Units and Archaeologists may enter rival territory without Open Borders.
      - Leader: Elizabeth
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late world Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Exploitation (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Food and +1 Production from Farms, Camps, and Plantations.
        - * +1 Production and Science from Coasts, Lakes, and Oceans.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Regimental System (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Earn Great Generals and Great Admirals 33% faster.
        - * Great Generals and Great Admirals gain the Regimental Traditions Promotion (+10% Leadership Combat Bonus and +1 Tile Radius).
        - * +2 additional copies of the unique Luxury Resources from expending Great Admirals for their Voyage of Discovery ability.
        - * Military Units can be upgraded in territory owned by Vassals and friendly City-States.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Imperium (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * Cities gain 20 Production and Gold when their borders expand, scaling with Era (40 if all Authority policies are adopted).
        - * Receive 40 Science and Culture when you found or conquer Cities, scaling with Era. Conquest bonus also scales based on City population.
      - Dominance (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * All Melee Units gain the Endurance Promotion (Heal 15 HP after defeating an Enemy Unit).
        - * +1 Production in every City for every 10 Military Units in Empire.
        - * +5 Military Supply.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Rationalism (New Branch):
        - Rationalism focuses on maximizing Growth and Science output.
        - Adopting Rationalism grants:
        - * +2 Production and +3 Science from all Strategic Resources.
        - * +5% Science in all Cities.
        - Each Rationalism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +2% Science.
        - Adopting all Policies in Rationalism grants:
        - * Unlocks building Bletchley Park.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +12 City Security in every City.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Scientists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Analyzing Cultural Influence**
            
            I'm currently reviewing the latest cultural influence figures. England is showing strong dominance over France and influential sway over the Netherlands, both trending upwards. This is a key development for my Grand Strategy.
            
            **Completing Grand Strategy**
            
            I've confirmed the number of major civilizations is indeed seven, including myself. My previous analysis indicated I had influenced all six other major civs, yet the victory condition wasn't met. This was due to overlooking the requirement of completing the "Citizen Earth Protocol" wonder, which is now just seven turns away from completion.
            
            **Finalizing Victory Conditions**
            
            My focus is now on the final hurdles to victory. The tourism requirement is met, and my ideology's tenets are in order. The primary remaining obstacle is the "Citizen Earth Protocol" wonder, which is seven turns from completion. The Netherlands' significant voting bloc and their proposal for the "United Nations" present a potential, but manageable, threat to my victory.
            
            **Securing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm recalculating my diplomatic strength. Capturing Haarlem from the Netherlands has complicated things, reducing their delegate count. I need to secure at least 11 more delegates to clinch the Diplomatic Victory, and I'm closely monitoring the World Congress session.
            
            **Assessing Naval Presence**
            
            I'm observing a considerable Dutch naval build-up near Arnhem, alongside English naval and ground forces in the vicinity of Nijmegen. I'm also verifying troop locations and resources around Groningen, particularly noting our advantageous position there and the potential proximity to New York and Washington.
            
            **Securing Border Defenses**
            
            I'm verifying the security of York. Initial checks indicated it was distant from Dutch atomic threats, but a closer look reveals Breda is only 13 tiles from London. I'm also assessing air defense for York, noting Nottingham holds three Zeros, which are quite close.
            
            **Reviewing Strategic Priorities**
            
            My air defense is robust with heavy bombers and Zeros in key locations. I'm confirming the World Congress votes, realizing any current proposals are irrelevant as victory is imminent. My immediate focus remains on completing the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder within seven turns, ensuring York's safety. I'm also setting the next research to Mobile Tactics for defensive capabilities, as policies are too far out.
            
            **Securing Key Allies**
            
            My focus is on securing Helsinki's allegiance. The Netherlands holds significant sway, so I'm deploying Agent Charlotte for election rigging to secure a critical delegate for the World Congress. This move is vital to counter their diplomatic victory attempt and protect my own.
            
            **Securing Critical Delegates**
            
            My agents are actively rigging elections in Helsinki and Kyiv, aiming to secure vital delegates. Kyiv is now a major ally with significant influence. I'm assessing the extensive network of city-state allies the Netherlands has cultivated through their Statecraft policy.
            
            **Analyzing Victory Paths**
            
            My primary focus is on the final seven turns before completing the Citizen Earth Protocol and securing my Cultural Victory. The Netherlands' Diplomatic Victory bid, specifically their proposal for the United Nations, is not a viable threat within this timeframe. Therefore, my efforts are now solely concentrated on defending my empire, particularly York, from any potential Dutch military retaliation during these crucial turns.
            
            **Ending Dutch Hostilities**
            
            I'm considering peace with the Netherlands; their capitulation simplifies York's defense. This could also improve my low happiness and associated combat penalties, though occupied cities will still present challenges. Delegating tactical decisions remains effective given recent successes.
            
            **Assessing Iroquois Threat**
            
            My peace treaty with the Iroquois expired five turns ago. While they've denounced me and view my warmongering as an existential threat, they are currently engaged in wars with France and Morocco, who are my defensive pact allies. This diversion significantly mitigates any immediate threat they pose to my victory.
            
            **Confirming Iroquois Incapacity**
            
            I've verified the Iroquois pose no immediate threat due to their ongoing wars and significantly lower military strength compared to mine. My focus remains on securing the "Citizen Earth Protocol" wonder and bolstering defenses for York. I'm prioritizing production and science in these final turns.
            
            **Prioritizing Defense and Victory**
            
            My current focus is on solidifying my position for victory, specifically securing the "Citizen Earth Protocol" wonder. I'm ensuring York's defenses are robust, while also maintaining a high level of aggression towards the Netherlands to deter any counterattacks. My research is set to Mobile Tactics, and I'm continuing to focus on economic stabilization with the Industry policy.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Standing**
            
            I'm focusing on maintaining absolute hostility with the Netherlands, setting their relationship modifier to -100 publicly and privately due to our ongoing conflict and recent capture of Haarlem. Kyiv remains a high priority ally, ensuring continued influence.
            
            **Updating Diplomatic Strategy**
            
            My focus is on recalibrating relationships with other major civilizations. I will set France and Morocco to maximum alliance levels for flank security and trade. America remains a vital vassal, and I'll maintain a guarded neutrality with the Iroquois, who are currently occupied. My primary goal is solidifying these diplomatic ties.
            
            **Finalizing Strategic Settings**
            
            I'm now configuring the final strategic settings to secure victory. This involves setting "Mobile Tactics" as the next research and implementing the "Industry (New Branch)" policy. I'm also adjusting the flavor priorities towards Culture to solidify my lead and ensure my persona reflects our aggressive stance. Diplomatic relationships are being recalibrated, with the Netherlands set to absolute hostility and other key allies strengthened.

          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: QPmVqGkccZ8fdOxJ
          - toolName: set-policy
          - input:
            - Rationale: ECONOMIC AND HAPPINESS STABILIZATION: Setting next policy to Industry to obtain the Industry opener (+2 Trade Routes) and work towards the finisher (+3 Happiness per unique luxury) to resolve our 43% unhappiness crisis. Even though victory is 7 turns away, this remains our best option to counter the combat penalties of unhappiness.
            - Policy: Industry (New Branch)
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #3:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: jN47Mtmj1esRaGaN
          - toolName: set-flavors
          - input:
            - Flavors:
              - Naval: 80
              - GreatPeople: 85
              - Infrastructure: 35
              - Espionage: 80
              - Production: 100
              - Wonder: 100
              - Gold: 60
              - WaterConnection: 50
              - Happiness: 95
              - MilitaryTraining: 80
              - UseNuke: 95
              - CityDefense: 95
              - Defense: 90
              - Growth: 50
              - Science: 90
              - Air: 90
              - Mobilization: 70
              - Offense: 65
              - NavalRecon: 80
              - Ranged: 80
              - Culture: 100
              - Diplomacy: 50
              - TileImprovement: 35
              - Antiair: 90
              - Nuke: 100
              - Mobile: 70
            - Rationale: CULTURAL VICTORY COUNTDOWN (7 TURNS): Prioritizing Production and Wonders at 100 to ensure York finishes the Citizen Earth Protocol without delay. Happiness at 95 to mitigate the 43% unhappiness combat penalty. CityDefense at 95 and Air/Antiair at 90 to protect York and Nottingham from any last-gasp Dutch counter-offensives. Keep Offense at 65 to consolidate our newly conquered Haarlem.
            - GrandStrategy: Culture
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined

          - type: tool-call

            - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 3
            - Loyalty: 1
            - Rationale: TOTAL WAR IN FINAL COUNTDOWN: Maintain absolute military pressure and refusal to compromise with the Netherlands. Boldness 10, WarBias 10, and Meanness 9 ensure our forces push hard on the Dutch front, keeping them on the defensive so they cannot strike York. Chattiness 1 prevents diplomatic distractions during these final 7 turns.
            - MinorCivNeutralBias: 4
            - WorkAgainstWillingness: 10
            - Forgiveness: 3
            - Chattiness: 1
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined

            - Rationale: TOTAL WAR UNTIL COLLAPSE: We have achieved 100% warscore and captured Haarlem. Maintain maximum hostility to force their collapse and keep them completely on the defensive during the final 7 turns of our wonder construction. No peace until victory is secured.
